## Rules applied (mechanical)

1. Read tools must be backed by the helper tier (`resolve` / `findBy*` / `search` / `getContext`).
   The registry currently carries 54 helper-tier tools, 0 read
   tools are still backed by a plain primitive — see "Helper-tier gaps" for the worklist.
2. `listAll` / `listPages` are never projected (unbounded reads).
3. Every list tool states its bound; every helper scan states its cap.
4. Mutating tools expose a `dry_run` input affordance.
5. `sensitive` / `requiresApproval` are stated on the tool.
6. No binary/download tool (resources photos, public_photos, uploads, exports, s3_exports) is projected.
7. `MCP_TOOL_OVERRIDES.json` is the only place a description may change; this script never re-derives one.
8. A curation `exclude` override (`{tool, field: "exclude", newValue: true, reason}`) drops a projected
   tool: one tool per distinct outcome, so no two tools in the curated list do the same job.

Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff). Do not use it to hide a record you may still need: Hudu has no archive endpoint for website (api-docs.json carries archive paths only for articles, asset_passwords, assets and companies), so this delete is permanent." | Description states the return shape, the impact bound, the dry-run affordance and the sensitive/approval flags (the registry purpose states none of them). |

| hudu_operations_searchKnowledge | name | "hudu_search" | Naming: one self-describing search tool. It is not a knowledge-base reader in two of its three modes (help, resources), so `hudu_search` is the shortest name true for all three; the backing operation stays operations.searchKnowledge (single-search-tool design, section 1.1). |
| hudu_search | description | "Search Hudu. One tool, three modes: mode=\"search\" (the default) runs the query; mode=\"help\" returns how to use this tool; mode=\"resources\" returns what is searchable and what each vendor text filter really covers. Only \"search\" needs query.\nmode=\"search\": ranked, fuzzy search over article TITLE and BODY content, asset names, custom-field VALUES and identifiers, plus company/user/group/website names. Each hit carries resource, id, a snippet, a 0-100 score band and the fetch call for the full record. As a TOOL it defaults to tier \"index\" (build or await the body index), so the answer is complete rather than body-blind.\nHonesty: limit default 8 / max 25, snippetChars default 200 / max 400 (0 = none); a bound that bites sets meta.complete false and names itself in meta.reasons. hits=[] is a complete \"nothing matched\" answer ONLY when meta.complete is true and meta.degraded is null - read degraded first: it says when bodies were not searched (\"body-not-indexed\") or the index was cold (\"vendor-only\").\nNot for identity lookup (hudu_find_* / hudu_get_*) or for paging (hudu_list_*). A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours; a missing query is a CONFIG_ERROR naming mode:\"help\", never a silent help fallback." | Description names the three modes and the default in its first line, the query requirement and the bounds, the tool-level tier:"index" default, the honesty contract for degraded/complete, and when NOT to use it. |

| hudu_search | inputSchema | {"mode":{"name":"mode","type":"string","required":false,"description":"Default \"search\" runs the query; \"help\" explains this tool; \"resources\" lists what is searchable. A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours.","enum":["search","help","resources"]},"query":{"name":"query","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only, and REQUIRED there (an empty call is CONFIG_ERROR, never a help fallback)."},"resources":{"name":"resources","type":"array","required":false,"description":"search mode only; default [\"articles\",\"assets\"].","items":{"type":"string","enum":["articles","assets","companies","users","groups","websites","asset_passwords","password_folders"]}},"topic":{"name":"topic","type":"string","required":false,"description":"help mode only; default \"core\".","enum":["core","all","query","limits","scoring","degradation","results","followup"]},"limit":{"name":"limit","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 1-25, default 8."},"snippetChars":{"name":"snippetChars","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-400, default 200."},"company_id":{"name":"company_id","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; one-company scope."},"updated_since":{"name":"updated_since","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; ISO 8601 lower bound."},"min_score":{"name":"min_score","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-100 band floor."},"exact_only":{"name":"exact_only","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true = no fuzzy matching."},"tier":{"name":"tier","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; tool default \"index\" (complete answer); \"auto\" = the SDK default.","enum":["auto","vendor","index"]},"refresh":{"name":"refresh","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true forces an index rebuild."}} | The tool's contract, flat and self-describing: one optional mode enum (search|help|resources, default search) plus the fields each mode honours, each field stating its mode, its bound and its refusal. Queried by the mode-honours-fields gate. |
| hudu_search | outputSchema | {"type":"KnowledgeSearchResult (mode-tagged union)","drops":[],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"mode","type":"string","enum":["search","help","resources"],"required":true},{"name":"hits","type":"array","items":{"type":"object"},"required":false},{"name":"meta","type":"object","typeName":"KnowledgeSearchMeta","required":false},{"name":"help","type":"object","typeName":"SearchHelp","required":false},{"name":"resources","type":"object","typeName":"SearchableResources","required":false}],"fullType":"SearchKnowledgeResult | SearchHelpResult | SearchableResourcesResult (exactly one variant per mode)"} | Each mode returns a distinct top-level shape keyed by mode, so a response cannot be misread as another mode's. |
| hudu_search | annotations.bounded | "mode=\"search\": limit default 8 / max 25, snippet default 200 / max 400, client scan capped; mode=\"help\"/\"resources\" are static reads with no vendor request" | The bound belongs in the tool's own annotations, not only in its prose. |
| hudu_get_article_context | outputSchema | {"type":"ArticleContext","drops":[],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"context","type":"object","typeName":"ArticleContext","required":true}],"fullType":"ArticleContext","expand":"expand: true returns the full ArticleContext"} | Output-schema trim (CORE token budget): the generated field list for this compact shape is replaced by a typed reference. The always-on CORE payload must stay under its 32,000-byte budget, and this record's field-by-field expansion alone was 2.4 KB of the tools/list a client re-reads every turn. Nothing is hidden: the description still states the shape and `expand`, and the full schema stays in the registry (`hudu_describe({operation:"articles.getContext"})`) and in capabilities.json. |
| hudu_find_asset_passwords_by_slug | outputSchema | {"type":"AssetPasswordSummary","drops":[],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"record","type":"object","typeName":"AssetPasswordSummary","required":true}],"fullType":"AssetPasswordSummary","expand":"expand: true returns the full AssetPassword"} | Output-schema trim (CORE token budget): the generated field list for this compact shape is replaced by a typed reference. The always-on CORE payload must stay under its 32,000-byte budget, and this record's field-by-field expansion alone was 2.4 KB of the tools/list a client re-reads every turn. Nothing is hidden: the description still states the shape and `expand`, and the full schema stays in the registry (`hudu_describe({operation:"articles.getContext"})`) and in capabilities.json. |
| hudu_operations_invoke | exclude | true | Duplicate outcome: the META tool `hudu_invoke` is the designed entry point for exactly this capability (it is what the catalog row and every META-tool description tell an agent to use), and both tools would run the same SDK dispatcher `operations.invoke`. Registering the plan row for `operations.invoke` is what makes the capability validated and checkable; shipping a second tool for it is not. A tools/list where two tools do the same job is worse than a slightly shorter list. |

| hudu_operations_invoke | operations.invoke | write | helper | Duplicate outcome: the META tool `hudu_invoke` is the designed entry point for exactly this capability (it is what the catalog row and every META-tool description tell an agent to use), and both tools would run the same SDK dispatcher `operations.invoke`. Registering the plan row for `operations.invoke` is what makes the capability validated and checkable; shipping a second tool for it is not. A tools/list where two tools do the same job is worse than a slightly shorter list. |

Restore one by deleting its `exclude` override record and re-running `npm run mcp:project`.

## Tools

### hudu_archive_article

- backingOperation: `articles.archive`
- description: Archive a Knowledge Base Article. Returns the written record, or the DryRunResult plan when dry_run is true. Impact: one record. Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff). Do not use it to erase: hudu_delete_article is the destructive path.
- example: `await hudu.articles.archive(1, { dryRun: true })`
- effect: `write`; flags: `idempotent`; permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: true
- annotations: effect="write", readOnlyHint=false, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=true, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, dryRunAffordance="dry_run", tier="primitive-write"
- inputSchema: `{"id":{"name":"id","type":"number","required":true},"opts":{"name":"opts","type":"object","required":false,"fields":[]},"dry_run":{"type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"Validate without issuing the write. Returns DryRunResult with simulated: true."}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"union"}`

### hudu_archive_asset

- backingOperation: `assets.archive`
- description: Archive an Asset. Returns the written record, or the DryRunResult plan when dry_run is true. Impact: one record. Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff). Do not use it to erase: hudu_delete_asset is the destructive path.
- example: `await hudu.assets.archive(1, 1, { dryRun: true })`
- effect: `write`; flags: `idempotent`; permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: true
- annotations: effect="write", readOnlyHint=false, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=true, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, dryRunAffordance="dry_run", tier="primitive-write"
- inputSchema: `{"companyId":{"name":"companyId","type":"number","required":true},"id":{"name":"id","type":"number","required":true},"opts":{"name":"opts","type":"object","required":false,"fields":[]},"dry_run":{"type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"Validate without issuing the write. Returns DryRunResult with simulated: true."}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"union"}`

### hudu_archive_asset_password

- backingOperation: `asset_passwords.archive`
- description: Archive a Password. Returns the written record, or the DryRunResult plan when dry_run is true. Impact: one record. Sensitive: it handles credential material — never echo a secret value into a log, a description or an error (the SDK redacts those fields by default). Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff). Do not use it to erase: hudu_delete_asset_password is the destructive path.
- example: `await hudu.asset_passwords.archive(1, { dryRun: true })`
- effect: `write`; flags: `sensitive`, `idempotent`; permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: true
- annotations: effect="write", readOnlyHint=false, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=true, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=true, requiresApproval=false, dryRunAffordance="dry_run", tier="primitive-write", sensitiveNotice="returns credential-shaped fields; SDK logs/audit payloads redact them by default"
- inputSchema: `{"id":{"name":"id","type":"number","required":true},"opts":{"name":"opts","type":"object","required":false,"fields":[]},"dry_run":{"type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"Validate without issuing the write. Returns DryRunResult with simulated: true."}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"union"}`

### hudu_delete_all_activity_logs

- backingOperation: `activity_logs.deleteAll`
- description: Delete activity logs. Returns the written record, or the DryRunResult plan when dry_run is true. Impact: BULK — removes every activity log from the given datetime on; there is no narrower endpoint, and an unbounded call is refused (POLICY_DENIED). Irreversible — dry-run first and prefer archive where the vendor offers it. requiresApproval: the MCP gateway must obtain explicit human approval before this tool runs; it is not safe to call autonomously. Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff).

### hudu_find_articles_by_slug

- backingOperation: `articles.findBySlug`
- description: Find one article by its URL slug. Preferred over articles.list for a slug lookup. Returns one record: a compact ArticleSummary (content, url, object_type, … dropped); expand: true returns the full Article. Bounded: limit default 25, max 100; the client scan stops at 500 records / 4 pages (RESOLUTION_TRUNCATED, never a partial answer). Do not use it to enumerate or to page: it returns at most one exact match (call hudu_search or hudu_get_article for that).
- example: `await hudu.articles.findBySlug('example', { limit: 1, expand: true, resolutionDetails: true })`
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false
- annotations: effect="read", readOnlyHint=true, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=false, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, tier="helper", bounded="default 25 results, hard maximum 100; client scan capped at 500 records / 4 pages", helperTierBacked=true, helperTierBacking="articles.findBySlug"
- inputSchema: `{"slug":{"name":"slug","type":"string","required":true},"opts":{"name":"opts","type":"object","required":false,"fields":[{"name":"limit","type":"number","required":false},{"name":"expand","type":"boolean","required":false},{"name":"resolutionDetails","type":"boolean","required":false}]}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"ArticleSummary","drops":["content","url","object_type","share_url","created_at","public_photos"],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"id","type":"number","required":true},{"name":"name","type":"string","required":true},{"name":"slug","type":"string","required":true},{"name":"company_id","type":"number","required":true},{"name":"folder_id","type":"number","required":true},{"name":"draft","type":"boolean","required":true},{"name":"enable_sharing","type":"boolean","required":true},{"name":"updated_at","type":"string","required":true}],"fullType":"Article","expand":"expand: true returns the full typed record"}`

Bounded: every sub-list is fetched with limit, default 25, hard maximum 100 — no sub-fetch walks the account. ### hudu_list_relations_for_endpoints

- backingOperation: `relations.findByEndpoints`
- description: List the relations that connect two records. Preferred over relations.listAll for the 'what is this record related to' question. Returns one record: a compact RelationSummary (created_at, updated_at, … dropped); expand: true returns the full Relation. Bounded: limit default 25, max 100; the client scan stops at 500 records / 4 pages (RESOLUTION_TRUNCATED, never a partial answer). Do not use it to enumerate or to page: it returns at most one exact match (call hudu_search or hudu_get_relation for that).
- example: `await hudu.relations.findByEndpoints({ type: 'example', id: 1 }, { type: 'example', id: 1 }, { limit: 1, expand: true, isInverse: true })`
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false

### hudu_resolve_any

- backingOperation: `operations.resolveAny`
- description: Resolve an identifier against several resources and return the candidates that match. Preferred when the caller has an identifier but not the resource type; use the resource's own resolve when the type is known. Returns ResolutionCandidateHit rows for each resource that matched, with scanned/truncated counts; it does not throw RESOLUTION_AMBIGUOUS across resources. Bounded: limit defaults to 25 and is hard-capped at 100; a larger value throws CONFIG_ERROR and is never silently clamped. Do not use it when the resource type is known: call that resource's hudu_get_<singular>.
- example: `await hudu.operations.resolveAny({ id: 1, name: 'example', slug: 'example', external_id: 'example', /* ... */ }, { resources: [], limit: 1 })`
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false
- annotations: effect="read", readOnlyHint=true, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=false, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, tier="helper", bounded="default 25 results, hard maximum 100", helperTierBacked=true, helperTierBacking="operations.resolveAny"
- inputSchema: `{"identifier":{"name":"identifier","type":"union","anyOf":["number","string","object"],"variants":[{"name":"identifier","type":"number"},{"name":"identifier","type":"string"},{"name":"identifier","type":"object","typeName":"IdentifierObject","fields":[{"name":"id","type":"number","required":false},{"name":"name","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"slug","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"external_id","type":"string","required":false},{"name":"domain","type":"string","required":false}]}],"typeName":"Identifier","required":true},"opts":{"name":"opts","type":"object","required":false,"fields":[{"name":"resources","type":"array","items":{"type":"string","enum":["companies","articles","assets","websites","asset_passwords","password_folders","groups","users"]},"required":false},{"name":"limit","type":"number","required":false}]}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"ResolutionCandidateHit","drops":["hits","truncated","scanned"],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"resource","type":"object","typeName":"R","resolved":false,"required":true},{"name":"id","type":"number","required":true},{"name":"label","type":"string","required":true},{"name":"item","type":"union","anyOf":["object","null"],"variants":[{"name":"item","type":"object","typeName":"SearchableSummaryMap[R]"},{"name":"item","type":"null"}],"required":true}],"fullType":"ResolveAnyResult","expand":"expand: true returns the full typed record"}`

### hudu_search

- backingOperation: `operations.searchKnowledge`
- description: Search Hudu. One tool, three modes: mode="search" (the default) runs the query; mode="help" returns how to use this tool; mode="resources" returns what is searchable and what each vendor text filter really covers. Only "search" needs query.
mode="search": ranked, fuzzy search over article TITLE and BODY content, asset names, custom-field VALUES and identifiers, plus company/user/group/website names. Each hit carries resource, id, a snippet, a 0-100 score band and the fetch call for the full record. As a TOOL it defaults to tier "index" (build or await the body index), so the answer is complete rather than body-blind.
Honesty: limit default 8 / max 25, snippetChars default 200 / max 400 (0 = none); a bound that bites sets meta.complete false and names itself in meta.reasons. hits=[] is a complete "nothing matched" answer ONLY when meta.complete is true and meta.degraded is null - read degraded first: it says when bodies were not searched ("body-not-indexed") or the index was cold ("vendor-only").
Not for identity lookup (hudu_find_* / hudu_get_*) or for paging (hudu_list_*). A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours; a missing query is a CONFIG_ERROR naming mode:"help", never a silent help fallback.
- example: `await hudu.operations.searchKnowledge('example', { scope: [], limit: 1, snippetChars: 1, company_id: 1, /* ... */ })`
- effect: `read`; flags: (none); permissions: `unknown`; dryRun: false
- annotations: effect="read", readOnlyHint=true, destructiveHint=false, idempotentHint=false, openWorldHint=true, sensitive=false, requiresApproval=false, tier="helper", bounded="mode=\"search\": limit default 8 / max 25, snippet default 200 / max 400, client scan capped; mode=\"help\"/\"resources\" are static reads with no vendor request", helperTierBacked=true, helperTierBacking="operations.searchKnowledge"
- inputSchema: `{"mode":{"name":"mode","type":"string","required":false,"description":"Default \"search\" runs the query; \"help\" explains this tool; \"resources\" lists what is searchable. A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours.","enum":["search","help","resources"]},"query":{"name":"query","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only, and REQUIRED there (an empty call is CONFIG_ERROR, never a help fallback)."},"resources":{"name":"resources","type":"array","required":false,"description":"search mode only; default [\"articles\",\"assets\"].","items":{"type":"string","enum":["articles","assets","companies","users","groups","websites","asset_passwords","password_folders"]}},"topic":{"name":"topic","type":"string","required":false,"description":"help mode only; default \"core\".","enum":["core","all","query","limits","scoring","degradation","results","followup"]},"limit":{"name":"limit","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 1-25, default 8."},"snippetChars":{"name":"snippetChars","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-400, default 200."},"company_id":{"name":"company_id","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; one-company scope."},"updated_since":{"name":"updated_since","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; ISO 8601 lower bound."},"min_score":{"name":"min_score","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-100 band floor."},"exact_only":{"name":"exact_only","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true = no fuzzy matching."},"tier":{"name":"tier","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; tool default \"index\" (complete answer); \"auto\" = the SDK default.","enum":["auto","vendor","index"]},"refresh":{"name":"refresh","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true forces an index rebuild."}}`
- outputSchema: `{"type":"KnowledgeSearchResult (mode-tagged union)","drops":[],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"mode","type":"string","enum":["search","help","resources"],"required":true},{"name":"hits","type":"array","items":{"type":"object"},"required":false},{"name":"meta","type":"object","typeName":"KnowledgeSearchMeta","required":false},{"name":"help","type":"object","typeName":"SearchHelp","required":false},{"name":"resources","type":"object","typeName":"SearchableResources","required":false}],"fullType":"SearchKnowledgeResult | SearchHelpResult | SearchableResourcesResult (exactly one variant per mode)"}`

### hudu_update_magic_dash_positions

- backingOperation: `magic_dash.updatePositions`
- description: Update positions of multiple Magic Dash Items. Returns the written record, or the DryRunResult plan when dry_run is true. Impact: BULK — rewrites the position of every item in the submitted array in one call. requiresApproval: the MCP gateway must obtain explicit human approval before this tool runs; it is not safe to call autonomously. Pass dry_run: true to validate without issuing the write (DryRunResult with simulated: true, the impact and the diff).
- example: `await hudu.magic_dash.updatePositions({ company_id: 1, positions: [] }, { dryRun: true })`

  {"name":"hudu_search","backingOperation":"operations.searchKnowledge","description":"Search Hudu. One tool, three modes: mode=\"search\" (the default) runs the query; mode=\"help\" returns how to use this tool; mode=\"resources\" returns what is searchable and what each vendor text filter really covers. Only \"search\" needs query.\nmode=\"search\": ranked, fuzzy search over article TITLE and BODY content, asset names, custom-field VALUES and identifiers, plus company/user/group/website names. Each hit carries resource, id, a snippet, a 0-100 score band and the fetch call for the full record. As a TOOL it defaults to tier \"index\" (build or await the body index), so the answer is complete rather than body-blind.\nHonesty: limit default 8 / max 25, snippetChars default 200 / max 400 (0 = none); a bound that bites sets meta.complete false and names itself in meta.reasons. hits=[] is a complete \"nothing matched\" answer ONLY when meta.complete is true and meta.degraded is null - read degraded first: it says when bodies were not searched (\"body-not-indexed\") or the index was cold (\"vendor-only\").\nNot for identity lookup (hudu_find_* / hudu_get_*) or for paging (hudu_list_*). A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours; a missing query is a CONFIG_ERROR naming mode:\"help\", never a silent help fallback.","inputSchema":{"mode":{"name":"mode","type":"string","required":false,"description":"Default \"search\" runs the query; \"help\" explains this tool; \"resources\" lists what is searchable. A field the mode cannot honour is a CONFIG_ERROR naming the fields it honours.","enum":["search","help","resources"]},"query":{"name":"query","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only, and REQUIRED there (an empty call is CONFIG_ERROR, never a help fallback)."},"resources":{"name":"resources","type":"array","required":false,"description":"search mode only; default [\"articles\",\"assets\"].","items":{"type":"string","enum":["articles","assets","companies","users","groups","websites","asset_passwords","password_folders"]}},"topic":{"name":"topic","type":"string","required":false,"description":"help mode only; default \"core\".","enum":["core","all","query","limits","scoring","degradation","results","followup"]},"limit":{"name":"limit","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 1-25, default 8."},"snippetChars":{"name":"snippetChars","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-400, default 200."},"company_id":{"name":"company_id","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; one-company scope."},"updated_since":{"name":"updated_since","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; ISO 8601 lower bound."},"min_score":{"name":"min_score","type":"number","required":false,"description":"search mode only; 0-100 band floor."},"exact_only":{"name":"exact_only","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true = no fuzzy matching."},"tier":{"name":"tier","type":"string","required":false,"description":"search mode only; tool default \"index\" (complete answer); \"auto\" = the SDK default.","enum":["auto","vendor","index"]},"refresh":{"name":"refresh","type":"boolean","required":false,"description":"search mode only; true forces an index rebuild."}},"outputSchema":{"type":"KnowledgeSearchResult (mode-tagged union)","drops":[],"dropsUnresolved":false,"fields":[{"name":"mode","type":"string","enum":["search","help","resources"],"required":true},{"name":"hits","type":"array","items":{"type":"object"},"required":false},{"name":"meta","type":"object","typeName":"KnowledgeSearchMeta","required":false},{"name":"help","type":"object","typeName":"SearchHelp","required":false},{"name":"resources","type":"object","typeName":"SearchableResources","required":false}],"fullType":"SearchKnowledgeResult | SearchHelpResult | SearchableResourcesResult (exactly one variant per mode)"},"annotations":{"effect":"read","readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true,"sensitive":false,"requiresApproval":false,"dryRunAffordance":null,"tier":"helper","bounded":"mode=\"search\": limit default 8 / max 25, snippet default 200 / max 400, client scan capped; ## Core profile (progressive disclosure)

The client-visible `tools/list` is a TOKEN BUDGET, not a security boundary. MCP has no
per-call schema fetch, so a host holds every registered tool on every turn; a reference
server therefore registers a CORE profile plus three META tools that make the whole registry
reachable on demand (progressive-disclosure design).

> **CORE inclusion rule (implemented in `scripts/project-mcp-tools.mjs`, not hand-listed):** CORE = every tool an agent needs to (R1) discover any capability, (R2) identify itself to the tenant, (R3) find a record it cannot name, and (R4) read one record of each workflow resource. A write is NOT in core: writes are reachable through `hudu_invoke`, which forces a dry run first and refuses a destructive operation without its confirmation flag. The workflow resources are the repo's own authored group A (scripts/derive-plan.mjs GROUPS.A), so the rule inherits an existing, versioned definition instead of a new opinion.

- CORE tools: 15 (3 META + 12 typed)
- workflow resources (group A): companies, articles, assets, asset_layouts, asset_passwords, websites, folders, password_folders, groups
- every write is OUT of core and reachable through `hudu_invoke`, which forces `dry_run: true` first

| # | tool | rule | backing operation |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | `hudu_catalog` | R1 discover/meta | — (serves the whole registry) |
| 2 | `hudu_describe` | R1 discover/meta | — (serves the whole registry) |
| 3 | `hudu_invoke` | R1 discover/meta | — (serves the whole registry) |
| 4 | `hudu_resolve_any` | R3 discover | `operations.resolveAny` |
| 5 | `hudu_search` | R3 discover | `operations.searchKnowledge` |
| 6 | `hudu_get_api_info` | R2 tenant | `api_info.resolve` |
| 7 | `hudu_get_company_context` | R4 companies | `companies.getContext` |
| 8 | `hudu_get_article_context` | R4 articles | `articles.getContext` |
| 9 | `hudu_get_asset_context` | R4 assets | `assets.getContext` |
| 10 | `hudu_get_asset_layout` | R4 asset_layouts | `asset_layouts.resolve` |
| 11 | `hudu_find_asset_passwords_by_slug` | R4 asset_passwords | `asset_passwords.findBySlug` |
| 12 | `hudu_find_websites_by_slug` | R4 websites | `websites.findBySlug` |
| 13 | `hudu_get_folder` | R4 folders | `folders.resolve` |
| 14 | `hudu_get_password_folder` | R4 password_folders | `password_folders.resolve` |
| 15 | `hudu_get_group` | R4 groups | `groups.resolve` |

### META tools (R1 — discovery of a capability that has no tool)

| tool | what it answers |
| --- | --- |
| `hudu_catalog` | every registry operation as a row: `op`, `tool`, `effect`, `requires`, `dry_run`, `reachable`, `reason` |
| `hudu_describe` | one operation's full schema, flags, errors and guidance |
| `hudu_invoke` | execute by canonical key, validated against the registry record before any request |

The catalog is GENERATED from `capabilities.json` (see `examples/tool-catalog.generated.ts`,
emitted by `scripts/build-tool-catalog.mjs`), so an operation that gains or loses a tool cannot
become invisible: every operation has a row, and an operation the escape hatch refuses has
`reachable: false` with the reason. An unexposed capability that is indistinguishable from a
missing one is the defect this closes.

Profiles a host may select (`HUDU_MCP_PROFILE=core|extended|all` in the reference server):
`core` = the 15 tools above; `extended` = the whole curated projection
(139 tools); `all` = both. The profile is a host knob, never a reachability boundary —
every operation stays reachable through `hudu_invoke` under any profile.
